Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any particles module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 927. The projective resolution has length at most 835 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_927(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 927. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^835(S, F) is trivial. QED.
